    ABOUT CGN MINING                                                       關於中廣核礦業
    The Company is incorporated in the Cayman Islands and listed on the    本公司註冊地為開曼群島，為一家在聯交
    main board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 01164). The principal    所主板上市的公司（股票代號：01164）。
    activities of the Group are the development and trade of natural       本集團主要從事天然鈾資源的開發與貿
    uranium resources used by nuclear energy enterprises, with a view to   易，旨在成為核電站天然鈾資源的主要供
    be one of the top suppliers of natural uranium resources for nuclear   貨商之一。
    power plants.

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                            業務回顧
The Group is principally engaged in natural uranium investment and         本集團主要從事天然鈾資源投資及貿易。
trading. As at 31 December 2022, the Company (i) held 100% equity          截至2022年12月31日，本公司旗下擁有(i)
interest in Beijing Sino-Kazakh, through which it held 49% of the          北京中哈鈾100%股權，並透過其持有謝
equity interest and off-take rights of products of Semizbay-U; (ii) held   公司49%的股權及產品包銷權；(ii) CGNM
100% equity interest in CGNM UK, through which it held 49% of the          UK 100%股權，並透過其持有奧公司49%
equity interest and off-take rights of products of Ortalyk. In addition,   的股權及產品包銷權。此外，擁有全資子
the Group had a wholly-owned subsidiary, CGN Global and held               公司中廣核國際銷售公司以及Fission公司
13.75% equity interest in Fission.                                         13.75%的股權。

Liquidity risk and financial resources                                   流動性風險和財務資源

The Company adheres to prudent capital and treasury policy and           本公司採納了謹慎的資金與財政政策及目
goals. During the Reporting Period, the Company’s operating fund         標。報告期內，運營所需的資金主要來自
was mainly from the cash generated from operating activities and         經營活動與外部借款現金。本公司現金需
external borrowings. The capital requirements of the Company mainly      求主要包括為收購天然鈾資源而可能發生
come from possible acquisition costs for acquiring natural uranium       的併購開支以及運營的資金需求。
resources and funds for operation.


The Group has sufficient financial resources for daily operation and     本集團有足夠財務資源應對日常經營業務
business and does not have seasonal borrowing demands. If any            所需，且沒有季節性的借款需求。若未來
suitable acquisition opportunity arises in the future, the Group will    有合適的併購機會，本集團將利用多元化
raise funds from diverse financing channels.                             的融資渠道籌集資金。


The Company’s financing capacity is affected by multiple external        本公司融資能力取決於外部和內部多種因
and internal factors. To obtain financing on more favourable terms,      素，為實現以更有利條件取得融資的目
the Company shall understand the external financing environment          標，公司需要瞭解外部融資環境，並基於
and adopt a reasonable financing model and strategies based on its       自身的資產負債結構而採取合理的融資模
structure of assets and liabilities.                                     式及策略。


The Company pays close attention to financial market trends, actively    本公司一直密切關注金融市場走向，積極
considers its responses to internal and external financial risks,        考慮如何應對來自內外部的金融風險，制
formulates reasonable models and strategies to ensure the safety         定合理的融資模式和策略，確保公司融資
and economic efficiency of financing, and takes strict management        安全性和經濟性，並採取嚴謹的債務風險
measures on debt risks to prevent exposure to related risks and to       管理措施防範公司面臨的相關風險，以促
facilitate the financial health and development of the core business.    進公司的財務健康及核心業務發展。


Financing model                                                          融資模式

Given the complex and ever-changing financial market, the Company        在複雜多變的金融市場環境下，本公司一
has been exploring diverse financing methods and strives to establish    直探索多元化的融資方式，努力建立短、
a financing model with combination of short-, medium-and long-term       中、長期資金相互搭配、直接融資與間接
capital, merged direct and indirect financing and multiple financing     融資相結合、多種渠道並舉的融資模式，
channels to secure stable funds. In the process of debt financing,       為公司提供穩健的資金保障。在債務融資
the Company has taken a balanced approach between cost and               過程中，本公司始終遵循成本和安全兼
safety. The Company aims to obtain competitive financing cost but        顧的原則。公司致力追求具有競爭力的融

Under code provision C.2.1 of part 2 of the Corporate Governance      根據企業管治守則第二部份守則條文第
Code, the responsibilities between the chairman and chief executive   C.2.1條，主席和首席執行官的職責應予
officer should be separate and should not be performed by the same    以區分，且不應由一人同時兼任。安軍靖
individual. Mr. An Junjing has been both the chairman of the Board    先生同時擔任董事會主席兼本公司首席執
and the chief executive officer of the Company. The Board believes    行官。董事會認為，安軍靖先生同時擔任
that Mr. An Junjing has served as both the chairman of the Board      董事會主席兼本公司首席執行官以確保領
and the chief executive officer of the Company to ensure consistent   導及經營的一致性。提名委員會將物色合
leadership and operation. The Nomination Committee will identify      適人選向董事會推薦出任本公司首席執行
suitable candidate(s) for making recommendations to the Board to be   官。
appointed as the chief executive officer of the Company.

Internal Control                                                         內部監控

The Board has overall responsibility for setting up and maintaining      董事會整體負責建立與維持公司內部監控
effective internal control system and for reviewing its effectiveness,   體系的有效性，並不斷檢討其效力以保障
to safeguard the Group’s assets and Shareholders’ interest. The          本集團的資產及股東的利益。董事會每年
Board conducts annual reviews to ensure that the resources,              進行檢討以確保公司在會計、內部審核、
staff qualifications and experience in relation to the Company’s         財務匯報職能方面以及與公司環境、社會
accounting, internal audit, financial reporting and the Company’s        及管治表現和匯報相關的資源、員工資
environmental, social and governance performance and reporting,          歷及經驗，以及員工所接受的培訓課程及
and training courses and related budgets received by staff are           有關預算是足夠的。公司各級管理層通過
adequate. The management of the Company at each level assesses           對內部監控系統的檢討來評估內部監控系
on the adequacy and effectiveness of the internal control system         統的充足性和有效性。內部監控部門每年
by reviewing it. Internal control evaluation department reports to       度向審核委員會匯報一次有關內部監控的
the Audit Committee once a year on key findings regarding internal       主要調查結果，審核委員會向董事會匯報
controls. Audit Committee, in turn, communicates any material issues     有關內部監控系統中存在的重大事項和問
regarding internal control system to the Board.                          題。

During the Reporting Period, the Audit Committee conducted a             報告期內，審核委員會已檢討內部監控
review on the effectiveness of the internal control system (including    系統的有效性（包括財務監控、運作監控
financial controls, operational controls and compliance controls),       及合規監控），並無發現任何重大監控失
and no significant control failings were identified. Therefore, the      誤。因此，董事會認為本集團的內部監控
Board considered that the internal control systems of the Group          系統有效且符合企業管治守則的守則條
are effective, and complied with the provisions of the Corporate         文。
Governance Code.

ANTI-CORRUPTION AND WHISTLE-BLOWING POLICY                                  反貪污及舉報政策

The Group does not tolerate any corruption, bribery, extortion, fraud       本集團於業務活動過程中並不容忍任何貪
or money laundering during the course of its business activities. As        污、賄賂、敲詐、詐騙或洗黑錢。因此，
such, it has formulated an anti-corruption and whistle-blowing policy       本集團制定了反貪污及舉報政策（「反貪污
(the “Anti-Corruption and Whistle-blowing”) which prohibits all forms       及舉報政策」），透過參考相關法律及法規
of corruption practice by making reference to the relevant laws and         禁止任何形式的貪污行為。反貪污及舉報
regulations. The Anti-Corruption and Whistle-blowing Policy forms an        政策構成本集團企業管治架構的重要一部
integral part of the Group’s corporate governance framework, which          分，當中載列本集團僱員須遵守的特定行
sets out the specific behavioural guidelines that the employees of the      為指引以打擊貪污。反貪污及舉報政策旨
Group must follow to combat corruption. The Anti-Corruption and             在為僱員及任何外部人士提供機密的舉報
Whistle-blowing policy is designed to provide the employees and any         渠道，以向本集團報告實際或存有懷疑的
external parties with confidential whistleblowing channels to report to     非法活動及企業財務報告、內部控制或其
the Group the actual or suspected illegal activities and misconducts in     他方面的違規行為。反貪污及舉報政策會
corporate financial reporting, internal control or other areas. The Anti-   定期檢討及更新，以與適用法律及法規以
Corruption and Whistle-blowing Policy is reviewed and updated on a          及行業最佳常規一致。
regular basis to align with the applicable laws and regulations as well
as the industry best practices.

Risk management and internal control                                          風險管理與內部控制

Comprehensive risk management                                                 全面風險管理

The Company has basically established a relatively comprehensive              本公司已基本建立起較為完善的全面風險
risk management system. With reference to relevant risk management            管理體系，以ISO31000: 2018標準《風險
requirements of the “Risk Management Guidelines” of ISO31000:2018             管理指南》及企業管治守則有關風險管理
standard and the Corporate Governance Code, the Company                       的要求作為重要參考，對重大風險進行有
effectively identified, evaluated and managed the significant risks.          效辨認、評估及管理，嚴格落實《全面風
It also strictly implemented the Comprehensive Risk Management                險管理制度》，並將全面風險業務劃分為
Policy. Meanwhile, the Company classified and managed the overall             重大風險、專項風險、項目投資風險、一
risk business, namely significant risk, specific risk, project investment     般風險，進行分類管理，建立了相應的風
risk and general risk, and established corresponding risk management          險管理流程。董事會、管理層、風險管理
process. The Board, management, the risk management department                部門及業務部門根據其於風險管理中承擔
and business departments were responsible for their relevant duties           的職責開展工作，董事會對風險管理及內
within risk management, and the Board was responsible for the                 部監控系統負責，有責任檢討該等制度的
risk management, the internal control system and the review of the            有效性，確保公司風險管理體系有效運
effectiveness of such systems to ensure the efficient operation of the        作。
system.

Such systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate the                 該等制度旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務
risk of failing to achieve business objectives, and can only provide          目標的風險，而且只能就不會有重大的失
reasonable and not absolute assurance against material misstatement           實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。
or loss.

     their personal career development plan, so as to help them to make          速成長。
     rapid career growth.

     Company established the internal talent market. Vacancies will first        公司建立了內部人才市場，空缺崗位優先

     internal human resources, thus to accomplish the development of             實現企業與員工共同發展。
     both the Company and employees.

     Company established a complete performance management                       執行過程中進行溝通、輔導、跟蹤及評

Cultivating talents                                                       人才培養

The Company always adheres to the concept of “cultivating prioritizes     本公司始終秉持「培養人，而不僅僅是培
training” and implements the basic requirements of “training,             訓人」的核心理念，落實「培訓－考核－授

all employees, authorized employment and lifelong education by            學習的基本要求，通過吸收借鑒國內國際
absorbing the experience and good practice of international talent        人才培養經驗和良好實踐，結合自身發展

and a standardized training management system by combining with           和規範的培訓管理制度。
its own development characteristics.


The Company formulates a detailed graded training plan every year         公司每年度制定詳細的分級培訓計劃，通
to improve the quality and skills of managers and employees at all        過面授培訓、網絡培訓、面授及網絡混合
levels through face-to-face training, online training, and face-to-face   式培訓的方式，提升各層級管理者和員工
and online mixed training, so as to effectively meets the demand for      的素質和技能，有效滿足了公司穩定發展
talents for the stable development of the Company.                        對人才的需求。


Remuneration system                                                       薪酬體系

Combined with the characteristics of the industry, the Company            為有效吸引高素質人才，激發員工潛能，
constructed a competitive remuneration system which appeals to            公司結合行業特點，搭建了富有競爭力
quality talents and motivates employees effectively. The remuneration     的薪酬體系。薪酬體繫著重體現崗位價值
system reflects the value of positions and individual contribution        和個人貢獻，鼓勵員工長期為企業服務，
and encourages long-term service of employees, jointly striving for       共同致力於企業的不斷成長和可持續性發
continuous growth and sustainable development of the Company and          展，同時共享企業發展所帶來的成果。薪
sharing the results of corporate development. Remuneration is mainly      酬主要由月薪、績效獎金、加班工資、津
composed of monthly salary, performance bonus, over-time pay,             貼與補貼和福利構成。
allowance and subsidy and benefits.

      The directors are responsible for the other information. The other        董事需為其他資料負責。其他資料包括貴

      conclusion thereon.                                                       的鑒證結論。


      In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial statements,    審核綜合財務報表時，我們的責任為閱讀
      our responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so,     其他資料，於此過程中，考慮其他資料是

      the audit or otherwise appears to be materially misstated. If, based      錯誤陳述。基於我們已執行的工作，倘我
      on the work we have performed, we conclude that there is a material       們認為其他資料有重大錯誤陳述，我們須

      The directors are responsible for the preparation of the consolidated     董事之責任乃按香港會計師公會頒佈的香
      financial statements that give a true and fair view in accordance with    港財務報告準則及香港《公司條例》披露規
      Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards issued by the HKICPA and          定編製該等綜合財務報表，真實而公平地
      the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance,         呈列其內容以及進行董事認為必要之內部

      to enable the preparation of consolidated financial statements that       於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述。
      are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.


      In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are     在編製綜合財務報表時，董事負責評
      responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going      估    貴集團持續經營的能力，並在適用情
      concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern      況下披露與持續經營有關的事項，以及
      and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors      使用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董事有意
      either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have no   將    貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其他
      realistic alternative but to do so.                                       實際的替代方案。

always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements       錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引

                     non-current classification is based on whether an

                     entity has a right at the end of the reporting period

                     to defer settlement of the liability for at least twelve              於報告期後至少十二個月
                     months after the reporting period. The amendments                     延遲結算負債。該修訂亦
                     also clarify that ‘settlement’ includes the transfer of               澄清「結算」包括現金、貨
                     cash, goods, services, or equity instruments unless the               品、服務或股本工具的轉
                     obligation to transfer equity instruments arises from a               讓，除非轉讓權益工具的
                     conversion feature classified as an equity instrument                 責任源自分類為權益工具
                     separately from the liability component of a compound                 的轉換功能，與複合金
                     financial instrument. The amendments were originally                  融工具的負債部分分開處

                                                                                           1日或之後開始的年度報告
                     or after 1 January 2022. However, in May 2020, the
                                                                                           期間生效。然而，於2020

                     beginning on or after 1 January 2023.

            the grants are intended to compensate. Specifically,                     益中確認。具體而言，首

            the Group should purchase, construct or otherwise                        建造或以其他方式收購非
            acquire non-current assets (including property, plant                    流動資產（包括物業、廠房
            and equipment) are recognised as deferred income in                      及設備）的政府補助於綜合
            the consolidated statement of financial position and                     財務狀況表內確認為遞延
            transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and rational               收入並在相關資產的可使

                                                                                     化基準轉撥至損益。


            Government grants that are receivable as compensation                    作為開支或已產生虧損的
            for expenses or losses already incurred or for the                       補償、或是以向本集團提

            Group with no future related costs are recognised                        獲得的政府補助，無未來

            receivable and are recognised as other revenue, rather                   間內於損益確認及確認為
            than reducing the related expense.                                       其他收益，而非減少相關
                                                                                     開支。

                             ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of                             預期信貸虧損為信
                             credit losses. Credit losses are measured as                            貸虧損之概率加權
                             the difference between all contractual cash                             估計。信貸虧損乃
                             flows that are due to the Group in accordance                           按根據合約應付本
                             with the contract and all the cash flows that the                       集團之合約現金流
                             Group expects to receive. The shortfall is then                         總額與本集團預期
                             discounted at an approximation to the assets’                           收取之所有現金流
                             original effective interest rate.                                       之差額計量。該不
                                                                                                     足之數其後按與資
                                                                                                     產原實際利率相近
                                                                                                     之利率貼現。

                             The Group always recognises lifetime ECLs for                           本集團一直就應收
                             trade receivables. The expected credit losses                           賬款確認全期預期
                             on these financial assets are estimated on an                           信貸虧損。該等金
                             individual basis for customer with significant                          融資產的預期信貸
                             balances, estimated based on the Group’s                                虧損是使用基於集
                             historical credit loss experience, adjusted for                         團過往的信貸虧損
                             factors that are specific to the debtors, general                       經驗就有重大結存
                             economic conditions and an assessment of                                的客戶作個別估
                             both the current as well as the forecast direction                      算，但須根據適用
                             of conditions at the reporting date, including                          於債務人的特定因
                             time value of money where appropriate.                                  素、整體經濟環境
                                                                                                     及於報告日對當前
                                                                                                     及預測方向（包括金
                                                                                                     錢時間值，如適用）
                                                                                                     的評估予以調整。

                             For all other financial instruments, the Group                          就所有其他金融工
                             measures the loss allowance equal to 12-month                           具而言，本集團計
                             ECLs, unless when there is a significant                                量與12個月預期信
                             increase in credit risk since initial recognition,                      貸虧損等額的虧損
                             the Group recognises lifetime ECLs. The                                 撥備，除非信貸風
                             assessment of whether lifetime ECLs should be                           險自初始確認以來
                             recognised is based on significant increase in                          大幅增加，則本集
                             the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since                     團確認全期預期信
                             initial recognition.                                                    貸虧損。評估是否
                                                                                                     應確認全期預期信

                                                                                                     是否大幅增加。

                     (iii)   Financial liabilities                                              (iii)   金融負債

                             All financial liabilities are subsequently                                 所有金融負債其後
                             measured at amortised cost using the effective                             採用實際利率法按
                             interest method. The related interest expense is                           攤銷成本計量。相
                             recognised in profit or loss.                                              關利息支出於損益
                                                                                                        確認。


                             Gains or losses are recognised in profit or loss                           收益或虧損於終止
                             when the liabilities are derecognised as well as                           確認負債時及於攤
                             through the amortisation process.                                          銷過程中於損益確
                                                                                                        認。


                     (iv)    Effective interest method                                          (iv)    實際利率法

                             The effective interest method is a method of                               實際利率法為計算
                             calculating the amortised cost of a financial                              金融資產或金融負
                             asset or financial liability and of allocating                             債的攤銷成本以及
                             interest income or interest expense over the                               於相關期間分配利
                             relevant period. The effective interest rate                               息收入或利息支出
                             is the rate that exactly discounts estimated                               的方法。實際利率
                             future cash receipts or payments through the                               乃為在金融資產或
                             expected life of the financial asset or liability, or                      負債的預計年期或
                             where appropriate, a shorter period.                                       （如 適 用）較 短 期 間
                                                                                                        內能夠精確地將估
                                                                                                        計未來現金收款或
                                                                                                        付款貼現的利率。

                                                                                               企業之權益

                     asset is estimated to be less than its carrying amount,              用價值之較高者為準）估計
                     the carrying amount of the asset is reduced to its                   低於其賬面值時，則資產
                     recoverable amount. An impairment loss is recognised                 之賬面值乃調低至其可收

      5.     CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGMENTS                                        5.   主要會計判斷及估計不明
             AND KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION                                             朗因素之主要來源
             UNCERTAINTY
             In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, the                應用本集團會計政策時，董事須就
             directors are required to make judgments, estimates and                   未能透過其他來源確定之資產及
             assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets and                      負債之賬面值作出判斷、估計及假
             liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.             設。估計及相關假設乃根據過往經
             The estimates and associated assumptions are based on                     驗及其他視為相關之因素作出。實
             historical experience and other factors that are considered to            際情況可能有別於該等估計。
             be relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.


             The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on                  估計及相關假設會按持續基準審
             an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                   閱。倘會計估計之修訂僅會對修訂
             recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if the          估計之期間產生影響，則有關修訂
             revision affects only that period or in the period of the revision        會於該期間確認，而倘修訂影響當
             and future periods if the revision affects both current and               前及未來期間，則會於修訂及未來
             future periods.                                                           期間確認。


             (a)     Critical judgements in applying accounting                        (a)   於會計政策中應用之主要
                     policies                                                                判斷

                     (i)    Deferred taxation on investment properties                       (i)   投資物業遞延稅項

                               For the purposes of measuring deferred tax                          就計量採用公允值
                               liabilities from investment properties that were                    模式計量的投資物
                               measured using the fair value model, the                            業的遞延稅項負債
                               directors of the Company reviewed the Group’s                       而言，本公司董事
                               investment property portfolios and concluded                        已審閱本集團投資
                               that the Group’s investment properties in the                       物業組合，總結為
                               PRC were held under a business model whose                          本集團於中國的投
                               objective is to consume substantively all of the                    資物業是根據其業
                               economic benefits embodied in the investment                        務目標是隨時間而
                               property over time, rather than through                             非透過銷售消耗投
                               sale. Therefore, in measuring the Group’s                           資物業所包含的絕
                               deferred taxation on investment properties,                         大部分經濟利益的
                               the directors of the Company determined that                        商業模式持有。因
                               the presumption that the carrying amounts of                        此，於計量本集團
                               investment properties measured using the fair                       於該等投資物業中
                               value model recovered entirely through sale                         的遞延稅項時，本
                               was rebutted.                                                       公司董事釐定以公
                                                                                                   允值模式計量的該
                                                                                                   等投資物業賬面值
                                                                                                   乃全部透過出售收
                                                                                                   回的假設不成立。

           (i)      Deferred taxation on investment properties                       (i)    投資物業遞延稅項
                    (Continued)                                                             （續）

                    As a result, the Group continues to recognise                           因此，本集團繼續

      22. TRADE AND OTHER RECEIVABLES                                            22. 應收賬款及其他應收款項
             (Continued)                                                           （續）

             The Group measures the loss allowance for trade receivables            本集團計量的應收賬款虧損撥備等
             at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs. The ECLs on trade                 於全期預期信貸虧損。應收賬款之
             receivables are estimated using an individual basis by                 預期信貸虧損乃經參考債務人過往
             reference to past default experience and creditworthiness of           違約記錄及信譽以及對債務人現時
             the debtor and an analysis of the debtor’s current financial           財務狀況的分析後採用個別基準估
             position, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,       計，並就債務人的特定因素、債務
             general economic conditions of the industry in which the               人營運所處行業的整體經濟情況以
             debtors operate and an assessment of both the current as               及對報告日期現時和預測狀況方向
             well as the forecast direction of conditions at the reporting          的評估予以調整。
             date.


             For other receivables, the Group measures the loss allowance           就其他應收款項而言，本集團計量
             equal to 12-month ECLs, unless when there is a significant             與12個月預期信貸虧損等額的虧損
             increase in credit risk since initial recognition, the Group           撥備，除非信貸風險自首次確認以
             recognises lifetime ECLs. The assessment of whether lifetime           來顯著增加，則本集團會確認全期
             ECLs should be recognised is based on significant increase             預期信貸虧損。評估是否應確認全
             in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since initial         期預期信貸虧損，乃基於自首次確
             recognition.                                                           認起出現違約的可能性或風險是否
                                                                                    顯著增加。


             There has been no change in the estimation techniques or               於當前報告期間所作估值技術或重
             significant assumptions made during the current reporting              大假設並無任何變動。
             period.


             The Group’s trade and other receivables that are denominated           本集團以相關集團實體功能貨幣以

   The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments          在本集團日常業務過程中，本集團
   in the normal course of the Group’s business are credit risk,          來自金融工具的主要風險為信貸風
   liquidity risk, interest rate risk and currency risk.                  險、流動資金風險、利率風險及貨
                                                                          幣風險。


   (a)     Credit risk                                                    (a)    信貸風險

           The Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk which                     因交易方未能履行責任而
           will cause a financial loss to the Group due to failure to            令本集團蒙受財務虧損之
           discharge an obligation by the counterparties is arising              最高信貸風險乃來自綜合
           from the carrying amount of the respective recognised                 財務狀況表所載列相關已
           financial assets as stated in the consolidated statement              確認金融資產之賬面值。
           of financial position.


           In order to minimise the credit risk, the management of               為將信貸風險減至最低，
           the Group has delegated a team responsible to ensure                  本集團管理層已委派一支
           that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts.              團隊確保跟進收回逾期債
           In addition, the Group reviews the recoverability of                  項之情況。此外，本集團
           each individual trade debt at the end of each reporting               會於各報告期結束時檢討
           period to ensure that adequate impairment losses are                  各個別應收賬款之可收回
           made for irrecoverable amounts.                                       性，確保就不可收回金額
                                                                                 作出足夠減值虧損。


           For trade receivables, the Group has applied the                      就應收賬款而言，本集團
           simplified approach in HKFRS 9 to measure the loss                    已應用香港財務報告準則
           allowance at lifetime ECLs. The Group determines                      第9號的簡化方法以按全期
           the ECLs for customer with significant balances                       預期信貸虧損計量虧損撥
           by using an individual basis, estimated based on                      備。本集團對具重大結存
           historical credit loss experience, as well as the general             客戶按個別基礎估算，以
           economic conditions of the industry in which the                      債務人過往信貸虧損經驗
           debtors operate. In this regard, the directors of the                 及債務人經營行業的整體
           Company consider that the Group has a low historical                  經濟條件釐定預期信貸虧
           credit loss experience there was no significant and                   損。就此而言，本公司的
           adverse changes in the general economic conditions                    董事認為本集團歷史信貸
           of the industry in what the debtors operate, thus the                 虧損為低，由於債務人經
           management considered the credit risk is not material.                營的行業之總體經濟狀況
                                                                                 並無重大不利變動，因此

40. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                              40. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the            本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團
   Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                 旗下實體能夠持續經營業務，並透
   maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation          過更完善平衡債務及權益，以為股
   of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy            東帶來最大回報。本集團整體策略
   remains unchanged from prior year.                                      與去年維持不變。


   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt,                本集團之資本結構包括淨負債（包
   which includes loans from a fellow subsidiary and loans from            括應收一間同系附屬公司貸款及
   immediate holding company, bank borrowings, net of cash                 來自直接控股公司之貸款及銀行
   and cash equivalents and equity attributable to owners of               借款），扣除現金及現金等值項目
   the Company, comprising issued share capital and reserves.              以及本公司擁有人應佔權益（當中
   The directors of the Company review the capital structure on            包括已發行股本及儲備）。本公司
   a regular basis. As a part of this review, the directors of the         董事定期檢討資本結構。作為檢討
   Company consider the cost of capital and the risks associated           其中一環，本公司董事考慮資本成
   with each class of capital, and take appropriate actions to             本及與各類資本相關的風險，並就
   adjust the Group’s capital structure.                                   此採取適當行動調整本集團資本結
                                                                           構。


   Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors              與業內其他公司做法一致，本集團
   capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated     以資本負債比率監察資本。該比率
   as net debt divided by total capital. Net debt is calculated as         以淨負債除以資本總額計算。淨負
   total borrowings less cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is       債按借貸總額減現金及現金等值項
   calculated as equity as shown in the consolidated statement             目計算。資本總額按綜合財務狀況
   of financial position plus net debt.                                    表所列的權益加上淨負債計算。


   The gearing ratios at 31 December 2022 and 2021 were as                 於2022年及2021年12月31日的資
   follows:                                                                本負債比率如下：


                                                                                    2022        2021
                                                                                  2022年       2021年
                                                                                HK$’000      HK$’000
                                                                                  千港元         千港元


    Net debt                                  淨負債                               2,348,970   2,943,188
    Total equity                              權益總額                              3,348,139   2,155,223

      釋義

      “USD” or “US$”                                         United States dollars, the lawful currency of the US.
      「美元」或「US$」                                   指         美元，美國之法定貨幣。

      “UxC”                                                  UxC, LLC, one of the leading providers of uranium prices and an
                                                             independent third party.
      「UxC」                                        指         UxC LLC，為鈾價格的領先提供者之一，並為獨立第三方。

      “Zhalpak Deposit”                                      the uranium deposit located in Sozak district, Kazakhstan, which is
                                                             owned and operated by Ortalyk.
      「扎礦」                                         指         位於哈薩克斯坦索扎克地區的鈾礦，由奧公司擁有及管理。

      *   For identification purpose only
